## Connection of arithmetic infrastructure to the source theorem

The selected arithmetic route has the following dependencies. The arrows below are **remaining construction obligations**, except where a named existing theorem is stated. They are not an already kernel-checked path from local reciprocity to a modular form.

1. Actual local cup product plus the invariant on its cyclotomic coefficient H2 → perfect finite-module local duality, including the trace-zero adjoint coefficient module.
2. Local duality, global reciprocity/duality, and the actual local deformation conditions → dual Selmer constraints and the numerical relation/generator bounds needed in patching. Existing finite-dimensionality or a bilinear cup alone does not give these conclusions.
3. Those bounds, the actual deformation rings and the remaining automorphic/patching construction → the automorphy-lifting input for symmetric-power potential automorphy.
4. Separately, construct the Galois representation of the actual primitive form and its Hecke/Frobenius and local compatibility. Patching does **not** supply this association by itself. Combine it with the required residual-image, regularity and non-CM hypotheses and potential automorphy.
5. Potential automorphy plus the required descent and automorphic analytic results → the literal higher symmetric-power continuation premise above.
6. Consume `primitive_satoTate_of_higher_continuation_alone` and the prime-bound reduction → DUB-17; consume `primitive_concentration_of_sato_tate` and the existing level-one bridges → DUB-18.

BLGG §§3.3–3.6 and Theorem 7.1.4 identify the automorphy-lifting/potential-automorphy route; Corollary 7.1.5 supplies its analytic continuation endpoint, and Corollary 7.1.7 is the frozen Dubon reference. This literature identifies the obligations, but supplies no Lean proof. Its purity and local compatibility uses must not be made circular by treating the analytic deduction of a prime bound as an upstream purity proof. [BLGG v2](https://arxiv.org/html/0912.1054v2)

The route correction is mathematical: if the global images are mutual annihilators, their restricted pairing is zero. `IsPerfPair.restrict` instead requires complements to the annihilators so that the restricted pairing is nondegenerate. Therefore use the existing perfect local pairing to transport a dual annihilator, prove the **reverse arithmetic inclusion**, and then apply annihilator dimension and quotient identities. Neither local perfectness nor global sum-zero proves that reverse inclusion. No gate or source hypothesis is removed by this search.
